# Pinned UI source inventory

Reference b30f95ac4a80d2f6dbe3aa01001b0524314f6d74. Developer audit only.
4183 tracked target files enumerated; 105 recognized assets default-excluded.
Unknown binary files are also excluded. Nothing here certifies runtime egress or fidelity.

The [raw inventory](../evals/results/ui-source-inventory.json) contains exact byte hashes,
all declared dependency versions, and line-numbered source candidates for routes,
components, themes, fonts, icons, network requests and Electron/IPC. Comments and
computed endpoints require manual tracing; this static enumeration is not a completed U0 audit.

Dashboard routes: sessions, files, analytics, models, logs, cron, skills, plugins, MCP,
pairing, channels, webhooks, system, profiles, profile builder, config, env, docs, chat.
The dashboard chat is PTY-based and is excluded; desktop chat is the target.
Desktop surfaces include chat/composer/sidebar, command palette, capability browsers,
settings subpages, approval overlays, onboarding, HUD, attachments, plugins and popouts.
Unsupported terminal, cloud, messaging, cron, plugin, pet and update flows are excluded.

Dashboard themes: default, default-large, nous-blue, midnight, ember, mono, cyberpunk, rose.
Desktop themes: nous, github, catppuccin, everforest, solarized, nous-alt, classic,
midnight, ember, mono, slate, cyberpunk. Third-party palette licenses remain to audit.
Brand names must change in product labels; required copyright notices remain.

Confirmed README rules: semantic primary/secondary/tertiary/disabled/on-accent text;
12px minimum; alpha >=0.7 without stacked opacity; text-display only on chrome;
component-scoped brand/body/page/wordmark/technical font tiers, no themed fonts on shells.
web/src/themes/fonts.ts generates Google stylesheet URLs: replace with bundled fonts.
desktop cloud-agent-session.ts invokes Portal bridge auth; exclude this flow.
Both upstream API clients and Electron bridges must be replaced by Sanctum contracts.
